STRATEGY (15-minute and 5-minute candles)
Read on the previous day, the latest completed session. Candles are labelled by start time: the last 15-minute candle is 15:15 (second last 15:00); the last 5-minute candle is 15:25 (second last 15:20). A stock matches only when ALL of these hold:
1) 15m trend: the 15:00 and 15:15 candles are in opposite trends (one Up, one Down).
2) 15m volume: the 15:00 candle's volume is higher than the 15:15 candle's.
3) 5m trend: the 15:20 and 15:25 candles are in opposite trends.
4) 5m volume: the 15:20 candle's volume is higher than the 15:25 candle's.
5) The 15m 15:00 candle and the 5m 15:20 candle have the same trend.
Trend = candle colour (close vs open); a flat candle has no trend, so it is never opposite or the same. Direction follows the final candles (15m 15:15 / 5m 15:25): Up is LONG, Down is SHORT.
Entry: the 09:15 open on entry day. Exit: 15:27.

Data notes: Candles come straight from Yahoo's 15-minute and 5-minute feeds. Prices are rounded to 2 decimals; zero-volume and corrupt candles are dropped and count as missing, which can only block a match. DATA CONFLICT means a native 15m candle's trend, or which 15m candle has more volume, disagrees with the same candles rebuilt from the 5m candles inside them, so the symbol is blocked rather than trusted. INCOMPLETE means no completed session (or none of the four candles) came back. STALE means the previous day differs from most other symbols. Each candle is shown as open→close so a signal can be checked against a chart.
